        "sparql": "PREFIX agents: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology#>\nPREFIX dcat: <http://www.w3.org/ns/dcat#>\nPREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>\nPREFIX eli: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/ontology#>\nPREFIX eli-dl: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/eli-draft-legislation-ontology#>\nPREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>\nPREFIX members: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology/members#>\nPREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>\nPREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>\nPREFIX skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#>\nPREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>\nSELECT DISTINCT ?committee ?label ?term ?start ?end ?termStart ?termEnd ?range ?period ?termType WHERE {\n  GRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/committees> {\n    ?committee a members:Committee .\n    OPTIONAL { ?committee members:committeeInHouseTerm ?term . }\n    OPTIONAL { ?committee skos:prefLabel ?label . }\n    OPTIONAL { ?committee members:hasCommitteeDateRange ?range . ?range a members:DateRange . OPTIONAL { ?range members:StartDate ?start . } OPTIONAL { ?range members:EndDate ?end . } }\n  }\n  OPTIONAL { GRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/houses> { ?term a ?termType . VALUES ?termType { agents:DailTerm agents:SeanadTerm } ?term dct:temporal ?period . ?period a dct:PeriodOfTime . OPTIONAL { ?period dcat:startDate ?termStart . } OPTIONAL { ?period dcat:endDate ?termEnd . } } }\n} ORDER BY ?committee ?label ?term ?start ?end ?termStart ?termEnd ?range ?period ?termType\nLIMIT 100",

        "sparql": "PREFIX agents: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology#>\nPREFIX dcat: <http://www.w3.org/ns/dcat#>\nPREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>\nPREFIX eli: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/ontology#>\nPREFIX eli-dl: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/eli-draft-legislation-ontology#>\nPREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>\nPREFIX members: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology/members#>\nPREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>\nPREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>\nPREFIX skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#>\nPREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>\nSELECT DISTINCT ?committee ?label ?term ?start ?end ?termStart ?termEnd ?range ?period ?termType WHERE {\n  GRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/committees> {\n    ?committee a members:Committee .\n    OPTIONAL { ?committee members:committeeInHouseTerm ?term . }\n    OPTIONAL { ?committee skos:prefLabel ?label . }\n    OPTIONAL { ?committee members:hasCommitteeDateRange ?range . ?range a members:DateRange . OPTIONAL { ?range members:StartDate ?start . } OPTIONAL { ?range members:EndDate ?end . } }\n  }\n  OPTIONAL { GRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/houses> { ?term a ?termType . VALUES ?termType { agents:DailTerm agents:SeanadTerm } ?term dct:temporal ?period . ?period a dct:PeriodOfTime . OPTIONAL { ?period dcat:startDate ?termStart . } OPTIONAL { ?period dcat:endDate ?termEnd . } } }\n} ORDER BY ?committee ?label ?term ?start ?end ?termStart ?termEnd ?range ?period ?termType\nLIMIT 100 OFFSET 400",
